GCTS research notebook · 6 November 2026 UTC

From a sealed pocket to a Wang proof

The geometry checker recovered exact areas from one Turtle cavity and two Penrose cavities. The shared translation-only Wang palette checked the corresponding residue refutations and three first-order arithmetic proofs. A reusable arithmetic lemma serves two geometrically distinct pockets.

Exact pocketsArithmetic lemmasWang squaresProof linesCheck the proofsReplay

Bind the arithmetic to measured boundaries

The geometry checker replays the placed tiles and their exposed pocket boundary. For the Turtle, the pocket has area \(9/5\) of a tile. For the Penrose cases, twice the pocket areas have coordinates \((1,3)\) and \((1,1)\) in the basis \((1,\phi)\), where \(\phi=(1+\sqrt{5})/2\) and the thin rhomb’s area is the unit. The required Penrose pockets lie within their fixed targets. The figures show the measured cavity and actual placed rhombs or Turtles around it. Turtle cores · Penrose pockets

The exact area bounds allow at most one Turtle, at most two rhombs in the regular pentagon and at most one in the concave pentagon. The finite proof permits blank slots and every tile type. Each increment preserves the measured coordinate modulo the tile increment. The demanded final residue is therefore impossible. The pooled two-pentagon control admits a residue model because summing the pocket areas removes the odd coefficients.

Use one arithmetic proof in more than one pocket

Let \(F_a\) be defined by \(F_a(0)=0\) and \(F_a(S(x))=S^a(F_a(x))\). The theorem asks whether any \(x\) can have \(F_a(x)=S^b(0)\) for a measured coefficient \(b\). These first-order goals quantify over all counts. The search selects ground instances by following predecessor cases and peeling paired successors. Propositional resolution constructs the actual proof. The checker also verifies the source clauses against the original first-order assumptions.

The regular Penrose pocket uses the proofs for coefficients \(1\) and \(3\). Both coordinates of the concave pocket use the same checked proof for coefficient \(1\). The Turtle pocket uses the proof for \(5n\ne9\).

In a replay of the 27 earlier Penrose requests, the search required a checked arithmetic lemma before accepting a cavity cut. It checked \(2n\ne1\) on the first cut and reused that certificate for the next 19. The 20 cuts and the search results match the earlier geometric run. Geometry still verifies that each cut applies to its current pocket.

Inspect the Wang squares for the final contradiction

Each colored triangle is an edge code of a square tile. Adjacent codes agree. The outlined square carries the tape head. The window shows actual verifier transitions from the final local proof fragment. Hover over a square for its edge codes.

Read each proof as formulas and derivations

Open a proof to read the original assumptions and each grounded clause. Each line also contains the exact boundary and record supplied to the shared checker.

First-order arithmetic

Coefficient 1 in steps of 2

Goal \(\forall x{:}\mathrm{Nat}\,\mathrm{F}(x)\ne S(0)\)

Original assumptions
  1. \(\forall x{:}\mathrm{Nat}\,S(x)\ne 0\)
  2. \(\forall x{:}\mathrm{Nat}\,\forall y{:}\mathrm{Nat}\,\left(S(x)=S(y)\Rightarrow x=y\right)\)
  3. \(\forall x{:}\mathrm{Nat}\,\left(x=0\lor \exists y{:}\mathrm{Nat}\,x=S(y)\right)\)
  4. \(\mathrm{F}(0)=0\)
  5. \(\forall x{:}\mathrm{Nat}\,\mathrm{F}(S(x))=S(S(\mathrm{F}(x)))\)

Check the logical records directly

The compact certificate carries the source translation, the dependency graph, and the exact Wang record strings. The logical checker decodes ground instantiation and resolution, checks each dependency against its earlier output, and requires an empty final clause. The separate transition replay checks the full geometric witness.

The compact checker rejected altered premises with their fragment identifiers recomputed, invalid dependencies, and altered source goals. It also rejected a valid certificate supplied for a different arithmetic statement.

After extracting the evidence archive, check the Turtle arithmetic proof with:

python3 -I -S -B source/logical_records_cli_v1.py verify input/coefficient-5-9.json data/area-coefficient-5-9-logical-v1.json

Shared Wang palette and portable search

Replay the geometry and proofs

The clean replay reconstructs the area inputs from the prior published geometry certificates, regenerates the proofs, and rebuilds the reader. The archive contains both the full transition witnesses and the compact logical records.
